local function SyncSingleSearchCardVisual(cEntry, theme)
	theme = theme or GetTheme()
	if cEntry.ToggleBtn and cEntry.GetState then
		local ok, st = pcall(cEntry.GetState)
		local isOn = (ok and st == true)
		cEntry.ToggleBtn.Text = isOn and "ON" or "OFF"
		FUI.TweenButtonState(cEntry.ToggleBtn, isOn and theme.Accent or theme.PanelBg, isOn and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or theme.TextPrimary, isOn)
	end
	if cEntry.JumpBtn then
		cEntry.JumpBtn.BackgroundColor3 = theme.PanelBg
		cEntry.JumpBtn.TextColor3 = theme.TextSecondary
	end
	if cEntry.SubLbl then
		cEntry.SubLbl.TextColor3 = theme.Accent
	end
end

FUI.RefreshSearchCardsVisuals = function(forceAll)
	if FUI.IsInitializing then return end
	local theme = GetTheme()
	if forceAll == true or (FUI.SearchResultsPage and FUI.SearchResultsPage.Visible) then
		for _, cEntry in ipairs(FUI.SearchCards) do
			SyncSingleSearchCardVisual(cEntry, theme)
		end
	end
	if (forceAll == true or ActiveTabName == "Favorite") and FUI.SyncFavoritesVisuals then
		pcall(FUI.SyncFavoritesVisuals)
	end
end
table.insert(ThemeObjects.CustomCallbacks, function() FUI.RefreshSearchCardsVisuals(true) end)

FUI.ApplySearchFilter = function()
	local rawQ = FUI.QuickSearchInput and FUI.QuickSearchInput.Text or ""
	local q = rawQ:lower():gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
	if q == "" then
		if FUI.SearchResultsPage then FUI.SearchResultsPage.Visible = false end
		if FUI.QuickSearchCountBadge then FUI.QuickSearchCountBadge.Text = "Search" end
		for name, data in pairs(Tabs) do
			data.Page.Visible = (name == ActiveTabName)
		end
		return
	end
	for _, data in pairs(Tabs) do
		data.Page.Visible = false
	end
	if FUI.SearchResultsPage then FUI.SearchResultsPage.Visible = true end
	if FUI.RefreshSearchCardsVisuals then pcall(FUI.RefreshSearchCardsVisuals, false) end
	local words = {}
	for w in q:gmatch("%S+") do table.insert(words, w) end
	local matchCount = 0
	for _, cEntry in ipairs(FUI.SearchCards) do
		local matched = true
		for i = 1, #words do
			if not cEntry.Keywords:find(words[i], 1, true) then
				matched = false
				break
			end
		end
		cEntry.Card.Visible = matched
		if matched then matchCount = matchCount + 1 end
	end
	if FUI.SearchEmptyLbl then FUI.SearchEmptyLbl.Visible = (matchCount == 0) end
	if FUI.QuickSearchCountBadge then FUI.QuickSearchCountBadge.Text = tostring(matchCount) .. " Found" end
	FUI.RefreshSearchCardsVisuals()
end

local _qsDebounceTok = 0
FUI.QuickSearchInput:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
	if FUI.QuickSearchInput.Text == "" then
		_qsDebounceTok = _qsDebounceTok + 1
		FUI.ApplySearchFilter()
		return
	end
	_qsDebounceTok = _qsDebounceTok + 1
	local myTok = _qsDebounceTok
	task.delay(0.04, function()
		if _qsDebounceTok == myTok then FUI.ApplySearchFilter() end
	end)
end)
FUI.QuickSearchClearBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	FUI.QuickSearchInput.Text = ""
	FUI.ApplySearchFilter()
end)

FUI.SwitchToTab = function(tabName)
	if not Tabs[tabName] then return end
	local changed = (ActiveTabName ~= tabName)
	ActiveTabName = tabName
	if FUI.QuickSearchInput and FUI.QuickSearchInput.Text ~= "" then
		FUI.QuickSearchInput.Text = ""
	end
	if FUI.SearchResultsPage then FUI.SearchResultsPage.Visible = false end
	local theme = GetTheme()
	for name, data in pairs(Tabs) do
		local isAct = (name == tabName)
		data.Page.Visible = isAct
		FUI.SafeTween(data.Button, FUI.TI.TabBtn, {
			BackgroundColor3 = isAct and theme.Accent or theme.CardBg,
			BackgroundTransparency = isAct and 0.02 or 0.22
		})
		if data.Indicator then
			FUI.SafeTween(data.Indicator, FUI.TI.Dot, {
				Size = isAct and UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 20) or UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
				BackgroundColor3 = isAct and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or theme.Accent
			})
		end
		if isAct and changed and data.Page then
			data.Page.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 56)
			FUI.SafeTween(data.Page, FUI.TI.TabPage, {
				Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 46)
			})
		end
	end
	if tabName ~= "Avatar Changer" and genv._TR_StopAvatarHoverPreview then pcall(genv._TR_StopAvatarHoverPreview) end
	if tabName == "Favorite" and FUI.SyncFavoritesVisuals then pcall(FUI.SyncFavoritesVisuals)
	elseif tabName == "Player Radar" and genv._TR_RefreshRadar then pcall(genv._TR_RefreshRadar)
	elseif tabName == "Target Fling" and genv._TR_RefreshFling then pcall(genv._TR_RefreshFling)
	elseif tabName == "Aimbot & Visuals" and genv._TR_RefreshTargetAimbotList then pcall(genv._TR_RefreshTargetAimbotList)
	elseif tabName == "Script Executor" and FUI.SyncConsoleUI then pcall(FUI.SyncConsoleUI)
	elseif tabName == "Hacker Detector" and genv._TR_RefreshDetectorOrder then pcall(genv._TR_RefreshDetectorOrder) end
end

local function RefreshAllTabsVisuals()
	local theme = GetTheme()
	for name, data in pairs(Tabs) do
		local isAct = (name == ActiveTabName)
		data.Button.BackgroundColor3 = isAct and theme.Accent or theme.CardBg
		data.Button.BackgroundTransparency = isAct and 0.02 or 0.2
		if data.Indicator then
			data.Indicator.Size = isAct and UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 20) or UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
		end
	end
end
table.insert(ThemeObjects.CustomCallbacks, RefreshAllTabsVisuals)

local function CreateTab(tabName, iconText, order)
	local initTh = GetTheme()
	local isFirst = (order == 1)
	if isFirst then ActiveTabName = tabName end
	local TabBtn = Instance.new("TextButton") TabBtn.LayoutOrder = order
	TabBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 35)
	TabBtn.BackgroundColor3 = isFirst and initTh.Accent or initTh.CardBg
	TabBtn.BackgroundTransparency = isFirst and 0.02 or 0.2
	TabBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold TabBtn.TextSize = 12 TabBtn.ZIndex = 6
	TabBtn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	if tabName == "Favorite" then
		TabBtn.RichText = true
		TabBtn.Text = '    <font color="#FFD43B">★</font>  Favorite'
	else
		TabBtn.Text = "    " .. iconText .. "  " .. tabName
	end
	TabBtn.Parent = Sidebar RegisterThemeObject(TabBtn, "TextColor3", "TextPrimary") AddCorner(TabBtn, 8)
	FUI.AttachSpringAndRipple(TabBtn)
	local Ind = Instance.new("Frame", TabBtn)
	Ind.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
	Ind.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0.5, 0)
	Ind.Size = isFirst and UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 20) or UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	Ind.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	Ind.BorderSizePixel = 0
	Ind.ZIndex = 7
	AddCorner(Ind, 4)
	local Page = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame") Page.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -54)
	Page.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 46) Page.BackgroundTransparency = 1 Page.BorderSizePixel = 0
	Page.ScrollBarThickness = 4 Page.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	Page.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y Page.Visible = isFirst Page.Parent = ContentContainer
	RegisterThemeObject(Page, "ScrollBarImageColor3", "Accent")
	FUI.PageToTabName[Page] = tabName
	local PageLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout") PageLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
	PageLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder PageLayout.Parent = Page
	local PagePad = Instance.new("UIPadding") PagePad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 4)
	PagePad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 10) PagePad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 4)
	PagePad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8) PagePad.Parent = Page
	Tabs[tabName] = { Button = TabBtn, Page = Page, Indicator = Ind }
	TabBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		FUI.SwitchToTab(tabName)
	end)
	return Page end
local function CreateSectionHeader(parent, title, order)
	local Header = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	Header.LayoutOrder = order Header.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24) Header.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	Header.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold Header.TextSize = 14 Header.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	Header.Text = "   " .. title Header.Parent = parent RegisterThemeObject(Header, "TextColor3", "Accent")
	local Bar = Instance.new("Frame", Header)
	Bar.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
	Bar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, 0)
	Bar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 14)
	Bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
	RegisterThemeObject(Bar, "BackgroundColor3", "Accent")
	AddCorner(Bar, 3)
	return Header
end
local function CreateCard(parent, height, order)
	local Card = Instance.new("Frame")
	Card.LayoutOrder = order Card.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height) Card.BackgroundTransparency = 0.12
	Card.BorderSizePixel = 0 Card.ClipsDescendants = true Card.Parent = parent RegisterThemeObject(Card, "BackgroundColor3", "CardBg")
	AddCorner(Card, 8) local Stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke") Stroke.Thickness = 1
	Stroke.Transparency = 0.6 Stroke.Parent = Card RegisterThemeObject(Stroke, "Color", "Stroke")
	Card.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		local curD = Config.GuiDesign or "Default"
		local hoverTrans = (curD == "CyberHud" and 0.0) or (curD == "LiquidGlass" and 0.08) or 0.22
		FUI.SafeTween(Stroke, FUI.TI.CardHover, { Transparency = hoverTrans })
	end)
	Card.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		local curD = Config.GuiDesign or "Default"
		local restTrans = (curD == "CyberHud" and 0.04) or (curD == "LiquidGlass" and 0.24) or 0.60
		FUI.SafeTween(Stroke, FUI.TI.HoverOut, { Transparency = restTrans })
	end)
	return Card
end local RightNotifHolder = Instance.new("Frame")
RightNotifHolder.Name = "TR_RightNotifications"
RightNotifHolder.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1)
RightNotifHolder.Position = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -18)
RightNotifHolder.Size = UDim2.new(0, 286, 1, -80)
RightNotifHolder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
RightNotifHolder.BorderSizePixel = 0
RightNotifHolder.ZIndex = 90
RightNotifHolder.Parent = ScreenGui
local RightNotifLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
RightNotifLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom
RightNotifLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right
RightNotifLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
RightNotifLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
RightNotifLayout.Parent = RightNotifHolder
local activeFeatureNotifs = {}
local activeNotifOrder = {}
local notifCounter = 0

local function DismissNotifEntry(nameKey, entry)
	if not entry then return end
	if activeFeatureNotifs[nameKey] == entry then
		activeFeatureNotifs[nameKey] = nil
	end
	for i = #activeNotifOrder, 1, -1 do
		if activeNotifOrder[i] == nameKey then
			table.remove(activeNotifOrder, i)
			break
		end
	end
	if entry.Inner and entry.Inner.Parent then
		FUI.SafeTween(entry.Scale, FUI.TI.NotifScaleOut, { Scale = 0.90 })
		local tw = TweenService:Create(entry.Inner, FUI.TI.NotifOut, { Position = UDim2.new(1, 310, 0, 0) })
		tw:Play()
		tw.Completed:Connect(function()
			if entry.Slot and entry.Slot.Parent then entry.Slot:Destroy() end
		end)
	elseif entry.Slot and entry.Slot.Parent then
		entry.Slot:Destroy()
	end
end

FUI.ClearAllNotifications = function()
	for k, entry in pairs(activeFeatureNotifs) do
		entry.Token = (entry.Token or 0) + 1
		if entry.Slot and entry.Slot.Parent then pcall(function() entry.Slot:Destroy() end) end
		activeFeatureNotifs[k] = nil
	end
	table.clear(activeNotifOrder)
end

NotifyFeatureToggle = function(rawName, isEnabled)
	if FUI and FUI.RefreshSearchCardsVisuals then pcall(FUI.RefreshSearchCardsVisuals) end
	if Config.ToggleNotifsEnabled == false then return end
	if isClosingPermanently or not ScreenGui or not ScreenGui.Parent then return end
	local cleanName = tostring(rawName or "Feature"):gsub("%s*%b()$", "")
	if cleanName == "" then cleanName = tostring(rawName or "Feature") end
	local stateBool = (isEnabled == true)
	local statusColor = stateBool and Color3.fromRGB(46, 224, 130) or Color3.fromRGB(242, 78, 88)
	local statusGlow = stateBool and Color3.fromRGB(135, 255, 195) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 145, 152)
	local statusText = stateBool and "ON" or "OFF"
	local subText = stateBool and (E.Sparkle .. " ACTIVATED  |  T.R V95") or (E.Dash .. " DEACTIVATED  |  T.R V95")
	local iconGlyph = stateBool and E.Check or E.Close
	local theme = GetTheme()
	local curDesign = Config.GuiDesign or "Default"
	local cardTrans = (curDesign == "LiquidGlass" and 0.34) or (curDesign == "CyberHud" and 0.04) or 0.06
	local cornerRad = (curDesign == "LiquidGlass" and 13) or (curDesign == "CyberHud" and 7) or 10
	local toastDur = 2.15

	local existing = activeFeatureNotifs[cleanName]
	if existing and existing.Slot and existing.Slot.Parent then
		existing.Token = existing.Token + 1
		local myToken = existing.Token
		existing.Bar.BackgroundColor3 = statusColor
		existing.IconPod.BackgroundColor3 = statusColor
		existing.IconLbl.Text = iconGlyph
		existing.Badge.BackgroundColor3 = statusColor
		existing.Badge.Text = statusText
		existing.Title.Text = cleanName
		existing.Sub.Text = subText
		existing.Sub.TextColor3 = statusGlow
		existing.Progress.BackgroundColor3 = statusColor
		existing.Progress.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		existing.StrokeGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, statusColor),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, theme.Accent)
		})
		existing.BgGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, theme.MainBg:Lerp(statusColor, 0.16)),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, theme.MainBg),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, theme.MainBg:Lerp(theme.Accent, 0.14))
		})
		existing.Scale.Scale = 1.05
		FUI.SafeTween(existing.Scale, FUI.TI.ToggleKnob, { Scale = 1.0 })
		FUI.SafeTween(existing.Inner, FUI.TI.NotifRefresh, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0) })
		FUI.SafeTween(existing.Progress, FUI.TI.NotifDrain, { Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0) })
		task.delay(toastDur, function()
			if activeFeatureNotifs[cleanName] == existing and existing.Token == myToken then
				DismissNotifEntry(cleanName, existing)
			end
		end)
		return
	end

	while #activeNotifOrder >= 5 do
		local oldestKey = table.remove(activeNotifOrder, 1)
		local oldestEntry = oldestKey and activeFeatureNotifs[oldestKey]
		if oldestEntry then
			oldestEntry.Token = (oldestEntry.Token or 0) + 1
			DismissNotifEntry(oldestKey, oldestEntry)
		end
	end

	notifCounter = notifCounter + 1
	local slot = Instance.new("Frame")
	slot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 282, 0, 54)
	slot.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	slot.BorderSizePixel = 0
	slot.LayoutOrder = notifCounter
	slot.ZIndex = 91
	slot.Parent = RightNotifHolder

	local inner = Instance.new("Frame")
	inner.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	inner.Position = UDim2.new(1, 310, 0, 0)
	inner.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	inner.BackgroundTransparency = cardTrans
	inner.BorderSizePixel = 0
	inner.ClipsDescendants = true
	inner.ZIndex = 91
	inner.Parent = slot
	AddCorner(inner, cornerRad)

	local scale = Instance.new("UIScale", inner)
	scale.Scale = 0.88

	local bgGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient", inner)
	bgGrad.Rotation = 18
	bgGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, theme.MainBg:Lerp(statusColor, 0.16)),
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, theme.MainBg),
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, theme.MainBg:Lerp(theme.Accent, 0.14))
	})

	local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", inner)
	stroke.Thickness = (curDesign == "CyberHud" and 2.0) or 1.5
	stroke.Transparency = 0.12
	stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	local strokeGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient", stroke)
	strokeGrad.Rotation = 25
	strokeGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, statusColor),
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, theme.Accent)
	})

	local bar = Instance.new("Frame", inner)
	bar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 4, 1, -14)
	bar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 7)
	bar.BackgroundColor3 = statusColor
	bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
	bar.ZIndex = 93
	AddCorner(bar, 4)

	local iconPod = Instance.new("Frame", inner)
	iconPod.Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 28)
	iconPod.Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0.5, -15)
	iconPod.BackgroundColor3 = statusColor
	iconPod.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
	iconPod.BorderSizePixel = 0
	iconPod.ZIndex = 93
	AddCorner(iconPod, 8)
	local iconLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", iconPod)
	iconLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	iconLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	iconLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	iconLbl.TextSize = 14
	iconLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	iconLbl.Text = iconGlyph
	iconLbl.ZIndex = 94

	local titleLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", inner)
	titleLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -106, 0, 20)
	titleLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 52, 0, 8)
	titleLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	titleLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	titleLbl.TextSize = 12
	titleLbl.TextColor3 = theme.TextPrimary
	titleLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	titleLbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
	titleLbl.Text = cleanName
	titleLbl.ZIndex = 93

	local subLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", inner)
	subLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -106, 0, 15)
	subLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 52, 0, 28)
	subLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	subLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
	subLbl.TextSize = 9
	subLbl.TextColor3 = statusGlow
	subLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	subLbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
	subLbl.Text = subText
	subLbl.ZIndex = 93

	local badge = Instance.new("TextLabel", inner)
	badge.Size = UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 22)
	badge.Position = UDim2.new(1, -52, 0.5, -12)
	badge.BackgroundColor3 = statusColor
	badge.BorderSizePixel = 0
	badge.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	badge.TextSize = 10
	badge.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	badge.Text = statusText
	badge.ZIndex = 93
	AddCorner(badge, 11)

	local progTrack = Instance.new("Frame", inner)
	progTrack.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 3)
	progTrack.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 1, -5)
	progTrack.BackgroundColor3 = theme.PanelBg
	progTrack.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
	progTrack.BorderSizePixel = 0
	progTrack.ClipsDescendants = true
	progTrack.ZIndex = 93
	AddCorner(progTrack, 2)
	local progFill = Instance.new("Frame", progTrack)
	progFill.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	progFill.BackgroundColor3 = statusColor
	progFill.BorderSizePixel = 0
	progFill.ZIndex = 94
	AddCorner(progFill, 2)

	local entry = {
		Slot = slot, Inner = inner, Scale = scale, BgGrad = bgGrad, StrokeGrad = strokeGrad,
		Bar = bar, IconPod = iconPod, IconLbl = iconLbl, Badge = badge, Title = titleLbl,
		Sub = subLbl, Progress = progFill, Token = 1
	}
	activeFeatureNotifs[cleanName] = entry
	table.insert(activeNotifOrder, cleanName)

	FUI.SafeTween(inner, FUI.TI.NotifIn, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0) })
	FUI.SafeTween(scale, FUI.TI.NotifIn, { Scale = 1.0 })
	FUI.SafeTween(progFill, FUI.TI.NotifDrain, { Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0) })

	task.delay(toastDur, function()
		if activeFeatureNotifs[cleanName] == entry and entry.Token == 1 then
			DismissNotifEntry(cleanName, entry)
		end
	end)
end

local STAR_EMPTY = utf8.char(0x2606)
local STAR_FILLED = utf8.char(0x2605)
local STAR_GOLD = Color3.fromRGB(255, 210, 45)
FUI.FavoriteRegistry = {}
FUI.FavoriteOrder = {}
FUI.StarButtons = {}
FUI.FavoriteCards = {}

FUI.IsFunctionFavorited = function(funcName)
	if type(savedConfig.FavoriteFunctions) ~= "table" then savedConfig.FavoriteFunctions = {} end
	return savedConfig.FavoriteFunctions[funcName] == true
end

FUI.SyncAllStarButtons = function(funcName)
	local th = GetTheme()
	local function updateList(nameKey, list)
		local isFav = FUI.IsFunctionFavorited(nameKey)
		local writeIdx = 0
		for i = 1, #list do
			local btn = list[i]
			if btn and btn.Parent then
				writeIdx = writeIdx + 1
				if writeIdx ~= i then list[writeIdx] = btn end
				btn.Text = isFav and STAR_FILLED or STAR_EMPTY
				btn.TextColor3 = isFav and STAR_GOLD or th.TextSecondary
			end
		end
		for i = #list, writeIdx + 1, -1 do list[i] = nil end
	end
	if funcName and FUI.StarButtons[funcName] then
		updateList(funcName, FUI.StarButtons[funcName])
	else
		for k, list in pairs(FUI.StarButtons) do
			updateList(k, list)
		end
	end
end
table.insert(ThemeObjects.CustomCallbacks, function() FUI.SyncAllStarButtons(nil) end)

FUI.ToggleFavoriteFunction = function(funcName)
	if not funcName or funcName == "" then return end
	if type(savedConfig.FavoriteFunctions) ~= "table" then savedConfig.FavoriteFunctions = {} end
	local nowFav = not (savedConfig.FavoriteFunctions[funcName] == true)
	if nowFav then
		savedConfig.FavoriteFunctions[funcName] = true
	else
		savedConfig.FavoriteFunctions[funcName] = nil
	end
	FUI.SyncAllStarButtons(funcName)
	if FUI.RebuildFavoritesTab then pcall(FUI.RebuildFavoritesTab) end
	SaveConfig(false)
	if NotifyFeatureToggle then
		NotifyFeatureToggle((nowFav and "Favorited: " or "Unfavorited: ") .. tostring(funcName), nowFav)
	end
end

FUI.CreateFavoriteStarButton = function(parent, funcName, pos, size)
	local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
	btn.Name = "TR_FavStarBtn"
	btn.Size = size or UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 26)
	btn.Position = pos or UDim2.new(1, -128, 0.5, -13)
	btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
	btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	btn.TextSize = 17
	btn.AutoButtonColor = false
	btn.ZIndex = (parent and parent.ZIndex or 2) + 3
	btn.Parent = parent
	local sc = Instance.new("UIScale", btn)
	sc.Scale = 1
	if not FUI.StarButtons[funcName] then FUI.StarButtons[funcName] = {} end
	table.insert(FUI.StarButtons[funcName], btn)
	local isFav = FUI.IsFunctionFavorited(funcName)
	btn.Text = isFav and STAR_FILLED or STAR_EMPTY
	btn.TextColor3 = isFav and STAR_GOLD or GetTheme().TextSecondary
	btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		sc.Scale = 1.35
		FUI.SafeTween(sc, FUI.TI.ToggleKnob, { Scale = 1.0 })
		FUI.ToggleFavoriteFunction(funcName)
	end)
	return btn
end

FUI.SyncFavoritesVisuals = function()
	if FUI.IsInitializing then return end
	local theme = GetTheme()
	for _, fEntry in ipairs(FUI.FavoriteCards) do
		if fEntry.ToggleBtn and fEntry.ToggleBtn.Parent and fEntry.GetState then
			local ok, st = pcall(fEntry.GetState)
			local isOn = (ok and st == true)
			fEntry.ToggleBtn.Text = isOn and "ON" or "OFF"
			FUI.TweenButtonState(fEntry.ToggleBtn, isOn and theme.Accent or theme.PanelBg, isOn and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or theme.TextPrimary, isOn)
		end
		if fEntry.JumpBtn and fEntry.JumpBtn.Parent then
			fEntry.JumpBtn.BackgroundColor3 = theme.PanelBg
			fEntry.JumpBtn.TextColor3 = theme.TextSecondary
		end
		if fEntry.SubLbl and fEntry.SubLbl.Parent then
			fEntry.SubLbl.TextColor3 = theme.Accent
		end
	end
end
table.insert(ThemeObjects.CustomCallbacks, FUI.SyncFavoritesVisuals)

FUI.RegisterSearchFunction = function(info)
	if not FUI.SearchResultsPage or not info or not info.Name then return end
	local idx = #FUI.SearchCards + 1
	local sCard = CreateCard(FUI.SearchResultsPage, 46, info.Priority or (100 + idx))
	sCard.Visible = false
	local titleLbl = MakeLabel(sCard, UDim2.new(1, -210, 0, 20), UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 5), info.Name, Enum.Font.GothamBold, 12, "TextPrimary", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left)
	titleLbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
	local tabStr = tostring(info.TabName or "General")
	local subLbl = MakeLabel(sCard, UDim2.new(1, -210, 0, 16), UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 24), "Tab: " .. tabStr, Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, 10, "Accent", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left)
	subLbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
	FUI.CreateFavoriteStarButton(sCard, info.Name, UDim2.new(1, -190, 0.5, -13), UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 26))
	local jumpBtn = MakeButton(sCard, UDim2.new(0, 62, 0, 28), UDim2.new(1, -160, 0.5, -14), "Open Tab", "PanelBg", 10)
	jumpBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if FUI.SwitchToTab then FUI.SwitchToTab(tabStr) end
	end)
	local togBtn = MakeButton(sCard, UDim2.new(0, 84, 0, 28), UDim2.new(1, -92, 0.5, -14), "OFF", nil, 12)
	local entry = {
		Name = info.Name,
		Card = sCard,
		TitleLbl = titleLbl,
		SubLbl = subLbl,
		JumpBtn = jumpBtn,
		ToggleBtn = togBtn,
		TabName = tabStr,
		Keywords = string.lower(tostring(info.Name) .. " " .. tabStr .. " " .. tostring(info.Keywords or "")),
		GetState = info.GetState,
		Toggle = info.Toggle
	}
	if not FUI.FavoriteRegistry[info.Name] then
		table.insert(FUI.FavoriteOrder, info.Name)
	end
	FUI.FavoriteRegistry[info.Name] = entry
	togBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if entry.Toggle then pcall(entry.Toggle) end
		if FUI.RefreshSearchCardsVisuals then FUI.RefreshSearchCardsVisuals() end
		if FUI.SyncFavoritesVisuals then FUI.SyncFavoritesVisuals() end
	end)
	table.insert(FUI.SearchCards, entry)
	SyncSingleSearchCardVisual(entry)
end

local function CreateToggle(parent, labelText, initialValue, order, callback)
	local Card = CreateCard(parent, 44, order)
	local EdgeBar = Instance.new("Frame", Card)
	EdgeBar.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
	EdgeBar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0.5, 0)
	EdgeBar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 0)
	EdgeBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
	RegisterThemeObject(EdgeBar, "BackgroundColor3", "Accent")
	AddCorner(EdgeBar, 4)
	local Label = MakeLabel(Card, UDim2.new(1, -146, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 0), labelText, Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, 13, "TextPrimary", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left)
	Label.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
	FUI.CreateFavoriteStarButton(Card, labelText, UDim2.new(1, -128, 0.5, -13), UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 26))
	local ToggleBtn = MakeButton(Card, UDim2.new(0, 86, 0, 28), UDim2.new(1, -98, 0.5, -14), "", nil, 11)
	local tCorner = ToggleBtn:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
	if tCorner then tCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) end
	local Knob = Instance.new("Frame", ToggleBtn)
	Knob.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
	Knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 20)
	Knob.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0.5, 0)
	Knob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	Knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
	Knob.ZIndex = ToggleBtn.ZIndex + 2
	AddCorner(Knob, 10)
	local StateLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", ToggleBtn)
	StateLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 1, 0)
	StateLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 0)
	StateLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	StateLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	StateLbl.TextSize = 11
	StateLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	StateLbl.ZIndex = ToggleBtn.ZIndex + 2
	local state = initialValue and true or false
	local function UpdateToggleVisual(animate)
		local theme = GetTheme()
		StateLbl.Text = state and "ON" or "OFF"
		local targetPos = state and UDim2.new(1, -24, 0.5, 0) or UDim2.new(0, 4, 0.5, 0)
		local targetTxtPos = state and UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 0) or UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 0)
		local targetBg = state and theme.Accent or theme.PanelBg
		local targetBarSize = state and UDim2.new(0, 4, 1, -12) or UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 0)
		if animate and isMenuVisible and not FUI.IsInitializing then
			Knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 20)
			FUI.SafeTween(Knob, FUI.TI.ToggleKnob, { Position = targetPos, Size = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 20) })
			FUI.SafeTween(StateLbl, FUI.TI.State, { Position = targetTxtPos })
			FUI.SafeTween(ToggleBtn, FUI.TI.State, { BackgroundColor3 = targetBg })
			FUI.SafeTween(EdgeBar, FUI.TI.Dot, { Size = targetBarSize })
		else
			Knob.Position = targetPos
			Knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 20)
			StateLbl.Position = targetTxtPos
			ToggleBtn.BackgroundColor3 = targetBg
			EdgeBar.Size = targetBarSize
		end
	end
	table.insert(ThemeObjects.CustomCallbacks, function() UpdateToggleVisual(false) end)
	UpdateToggleVisual(false)
	local function SetState(newState, triggerCallback)
		state = newState and true or false
		UpdateToggleVisual(true)
		if triggerCallback ~= false then
			NotifyFeatureToggle(labelText, state)
			if callback then pcall(callback, state) end
		elseif FUI.RefreshSearchCardsVisuals then
			pcall(FUI.RefreshSearchCardsVisuals, false)
		end
	end
	FUI.ToggleHandles[labelText] = SetState
	ToggleBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() SetState(not state, true) end)
	if FUI.RegisterSearchFunction then
		FUI.RegisterSearchFunction({
			Name = labelText,
			TabName = (FUI.PageToTabName and FUI.PageToTabName[parent]) or "Themes & Status",
			GetState = function() return state end,
			Toggle = function() SetState(not state, true) end
		})
	end
	return { SetState = SetState, GetState = function() return state end, Card = Card } end
local function CreateSlider(parent, labelText, minVal, maxVal, initialVal, order, callback)
	local Card = CreateCard(parent, 60, order)
	local Title = MakeLabel(Card, UDim2.new(0.65, 0, 0, 22), UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 6), labelText, Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, 13, "TextPrimary", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left)
	local ValueLabel = MakeLabel(Card, UDim2.new(0.3, -14, 0, 22), UDim2.new(0.7, 0, 0, 6), tostring(initialVal), Enum.Font.GothamBold, 13, "Accent", Enum.TextXAlignment.Right)
	local BarBg = Instance.new("Frame") BarBg.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 12)
	BarBg.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 36) BarBg.BorderSizePixel = 0 BarBg.Parent = Card
	RegisterThemeObject(BarBg, "BackgroundColor3", "PanelBg") AddCorner(BarBg, 10)
	local Fill = Instance.new("Frame")
	local initAlpha = math.clamp((initialVal - minVal) / (maxVal - minVal), 0, 1)
	Fill.Size = UDim2.new(initAlpha, 0, 1, 0) Fill.BorderSizePixel = 0 Fill.Parent = BarBg
	RegisterThemeObject(Fill, "BackgroundColor3", "Accent") AddCorner(Fill, 10)
	local Thumb = Instance.new("Frame", Fill)
	Thumb.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
	Thumb.Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0)
	Thumb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
	Thumb.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	Thumb.BorderSizePixel = 0
	Thumb.ZIndex = Fill.ZIndex + 2
	AddCorner(Thumb, 10)
	local ThumbStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", Thumb)
	ThumbStroke.Thickness = 2
	RegisterThemeObject(ThumbStroke, "Color", "Accent")
	local sliding = false
	local lastVal = initialVal
	local function UpdateFromInput(inputPosX)
		local relX = math.clamp(inputPosX - BarBg.AbsolutePosition.X, 0, BarBg.AbsoluteSize.X)
		local alpha = (BarBg.AbsoluteSize.X > 0) and (relX / BarBg.AbsoluteSize.X) or 0
		local value = math.floor(minVal + (maxVal - minVal) * alpha + 0.5)
		Fill.Size = UDim2.new(alpha, 0, 1, 0)
		if value ~= lastVal then
			lastVal = value
			ValueLabel.Text = tostring(value)
			if callback then pcall(callback, value) end
		end
	end
	BarBg.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			sliding = true
			FUI.SafeTween(Thumb, FUI.TI.HoverIn, { Size = UDim2.new(0, 21, 0, 21) })
			UpdateFromInput(input.Position.X)
			input.Changed:Connect(function()
				if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
					sliding = false
					FUI.SafeTween(Thumb, FUI.TI.HoverOut, { Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16) })
				end
			end)
		end
	end)
	if UserInputService.InputEnded then table.insert(Connections, UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			if sliding then
				sliding = false
				FUI.SafeTween(Thumb, FUI.TI.HoverOut, { Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16) })
			end
		end end)) end
	table.insert(Connections, UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if not sliding then return end
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
			if UserInputService.IsMouseButtonPressed and not UserInputService:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1) then
				sliding = false FUI.SafeTween(Thumb, FUI.TI.HoverOut, { Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16) }) return end
			UpdateFromInput(input.Position.X)
		elseif input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			UpdateFromInput(input.Position.X) end end)) return { SetValue = function(val)
			local clamped = math.clamp(val, minVal, maxVal)
			lastVal = clamped
			FUI.SafeTween(Fill, FUI.TI.SliderSet, { Size = UDim2.new((clamped - minVal) / (maxVal - minVal), 0, 1, 0) })
			ValueLabel.Text = tostring(clamped) end } end
local Tab1_Dashboard = CreateTab("Themes & Status", E.Chart, 1)
local Tab0_Favorite  = CreateTab("Favorite", STAR_FILLED, 2)
local Tab2_Waypoints = CreateTab("Waypoints & TP", E.Pin, 3)
local Tab3_Radar     = CreateTab("Player Radar", E.Radar, 4)
local Tab4_Items     = CreateTab("Items & Prompts", E.Gem, 5)
local Tab5_Flight    = CreateTab("Flight & Movement", E.Rocket, 6)
local Tab6_Instant   = CreateTab("Target Fling", E.Bolt, 7)
local Tab7_Visuals   = CreateTab("Aimbot & Visuals", E.Target, 8)
local Tab8_Executor  = CreateTab("Script Executor", E.Laptop, 9)
local Tab9_Detector  = CreateTab("Hacker Detector", E.Search, 10)
local Tab10_Avatar   = CreateTab("Avatar Changer", E.Crown, 11)

do
	local favHdr = CreateSectionHeader(Tab0_Favorite, 'Favorite Functions (Click <font color="#FFD43B">★</font> on any toggle across tabs to pin here)', 1)
	favHdr.RichText = true
	local FavTopCard = CreateCard(Tab0_Favorite, 44, 2)
	local FavCountLbl = MakeLabel(FavTopCard, UDim2.new(0.62, -14, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0), '<font color="#FFD43B">★</font> Pinned Favorites: 0', Enum.Font.GothamBold, 12, "TextPrimary", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left)
	FavCountLbl.RichText = true
	local ClearFavsBtn = MakeButton(FavTopCard, UDim2.new(0.38, -12, 0, 28), UDim2.new(0.62, 2, 0.5, -14), E.Trash .. " Clear Favorites", "AccentDark", 11)
	local FavListContainer = Instance.new("Frame", Tab0_Favorite)
	FavListContainer.LayoutOrder = 3
	FavListContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
	FavListContainer.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
	FavListContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	local FavListLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", FavListContainer)
	FavListLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
	FavListLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder

	FUI.RebuildFavoritesTab = function()
		if not FavListContainer or not FavListContainer.Parent then return end
		for _, ch in ipairs(FavListContainer:GetChildren()) do
			if ch:IsA("Frame") or ch:IsA("TextLabel") then ch:Destroy() end
		end
		table.clear(FUI.FavoriteCards)
		local theme = GetTheme()
		local count = 0
		for _, funcName in ipairs(FUI.FavoriteOrder) do
			if FUI.IsFunctionFavorited(funcName) then
				local reg = FUI.FavoriteRegistry[funcName]
				if reg then
					count = count + 1
					local fCard = CreateCard(FavListContainer, 48, count)
					local unstarBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", fCard)
					unstarBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 28)
					unstarBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0.5, -14)
					unstarBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
					unstarBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
					unstarBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
					unstarBtn.TextSize = 18
					unstarBtn.Text = STAR_FILLED
					unstarBtn.TextColor3 = STAR_GOLD
					unstarBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
						FUI.ToggleFavoriteFunction(funcName)
					end)

					local tLbl = MakeLabel(fCard, UDim2.new(1, -214, 0, 20), UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 5), tostring(reg.Name), Enum.Font.GothamBold, 12, "TextPrimary", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left)
					tLbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
					local sLbl = MakeLabel(fCard, UDim2.new(1, -214, 0, 16), UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 25), "Tab: " .. tostring(reg.TabName or "General"), Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, 10, "Accent", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left)
					sLbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd

					local jBtn = MakeButton(fCard, UDim2.new(0, 62, 0, 28), UDim2.new(1, -162, 0.5, -14), "Open Tab", "PanelBg", 10)
					jBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
						if FUI.SwitchToTab and reg.TabName then FUI.SwitchToTab(reg.TabName) end
					end)

					local okSt, curSt = pcall(reg.GetState)
					local isOn = (okSt and curSt == true)
					local tBtn = MakeButton(fCard, UDim2.new(0, 84, 0, 28), UDim2.new(1, -94, 0.5, -14), isOn and "ON" or "OFF", nil, 12)
					FUI.TweenButtonState(tBtn, isOn and theme.Accent or theme.PanelBg, isOn and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or theme.TextPrimary, isOn)
					tBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
						if reg.Toggle then pcall(reg.Toggle) end
						if FUI.RefreshSearchCardsVisuals then FUI.RefreshSearchCardsVisuals() end
						if FUI.SyncFavoritesVisuals then FUI.SyncFavoritesVisuals() end
					end)

					table.insert(FUI.FavoriteCards, {
						Card = fCard,
						SubLbl = sLbl,
						JumpBtn = jBtn,
						ToggleBtn = tBtn,
						GetState = reg.GetState
					})
				end
			end
		end
		FavCountLbl.Text = '<font color="#FFD43B">★</font> Pinned Favorites: ' .. tostring(count)
		if count == 0 then
			local emptyLbl = MakeLabel(FavListContainer, UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 42), nil, "No favorite functions pinned yet! Go to any tab and click the small " .. STAR_EMPTY .. " star next to any toggle to pin it here.", Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, 11, "TextSecondary", Enum.TextXAlignment.Center)
			emptyLbl.TextWrapped = true
		end
	end

	ClearFavsBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		savedConfig.FavoriteFunctions = {}
		FUI.SyncAllStarButtons(nil)
		FUI.RebuildFavoritesTab()
		SaveConfig(false)
		NotifyFeatureToggle("Cleared All Favorites", false)
	end)
end
local PerfLabel, CursorUnlockedOverride, SetMouseLockState, ActiveKeybindCaptureCallback, TryAssignUniqueKeybind
local GetRootPart, GetCharacterFromPlayer, GetCachedCharBaseParts, freezePosition, lastSafeAntiFlingCF, SafeTeleportToCoords
local function SetupTab1()
local RefreshMouseLockUI
local function FindKeybindConflict(keyName, ignoreSlot)
	if not keyName or keyName == "" or keyName == "None" then return nil end
	if keyName == "X" and ignoreSlot ~= "MenuToggle" then
		return "Toggle UI Menu [X]"
	end
	if ignoreSlot ~= "MouseLock" and (Config.MouseLockKeybind or "M") == keyName then
		return "Mouse Lock"
	end
	if ignoreSlot ~= "Flight" and (savedConfig.Settings.FlyKeybind or "E") == keyName then
		return "Flight Toggle"
	end
	if ignoreSlot ~= "Aimbot" and (Config.AimbotKeybind or "Q") == keyName then
		return "Aimbot Lock"
	end
	if ignoreSlot ~= "Freecam" and (Config.FreecamKeybind or "C") == keyName then
		return "Freecam"
	end
	if ignoreSlot ~= "TargetAimbot" and (Config.TargetAimbotKeybind or "T") == keyName then
		return "Target Player Aimbot"
	end
	if ignoreSlot ~= "AutoShoot" and (Config.AutoShootKeybind or "Z") == keyName then
		return "Auto Shoot"
	end
	if ignoreSlot ~= "RadialWheel" and (Config.RadialWheelKeybind or "V") == keyName then
		return "Radial Quick-Action Wheel"
	end
	if type(savedConfig.Waypoints) == "table" then
		for idx, wp in ipairs(savedConfig.Waypoints) do
			if ignoreSlot ~= ("Waypoint:" .. tostring(idx)) and wp.Keybind and wp.Keybind ~= "None" and wp.Keybind == keyName then
				return "Waypoint (" .. tostring(wp.Name or ("#" .. idx)) .. ")"
			end
		end
	end
	if type(savedConfig.RadarKeybinds) == "table" then
		for plrName, boundKey in pairs(savedConfig.RadarKeybinds) do
			if ignoreSlot ~= ("Radar:" .. tostring(plrName)) and boundKey and boundKey ~= "None" and boundKey == keyName then
				return "Radar Teleport (@" .. tostring(plrName) .. ")"
			end
		end
	end
	return nil
end
TryAssignUniqueKeybind = function(keyName, slotId, applyFn, refreshFn, btnObj)
	if not keyName or keyName == "None" then
		if applyFn then applyFn("None") end
		if refreshFn then refreshFn() end
		SaveConfig(false)
		return true
	end
	local conflictOwner = FindKeybindConflict(keyName, slotId)
	if conflictOwner then
		if btnObj then
			btnObj.Text = "[Used: " .. tostring(keyName) .. "]"
			task.delay(1.1, function()
				if refreshFn then pcall(refreshFn) end
			end)
		elseif refreshFn then
			pcall(refreshFn)
		end
		pcall(function()
			StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
				Title = "Keybind Already Assigned",
				Text = "Key [" .. tostring(keyName) .. "] is already assigned to " .. tostring(conflictOwner) .. "!",
				Duration = 3
			})
		end)
		return false
	end
	if applyFn then applyFn(keyName) end
	if refreshFn then refreshFn() end
	SaveConfig(false)
	return true
end
CreateSectionHeader(Tab1_Dashboard, "GUI Design Style (1. Default | 2. Apple Liquid Glass | 3. Neon Cyber-HUD)", 1)

local DesignButtons = {}
local DesignOptions = {
	{ Id = "Default", Label = "1. Default Classic" },
	{ Id = "LiquidGlass", Label = "2. Apple Liquid Glass" },
	{ Id = "CyberHud", Label = "3. Neon Cyber-HUD" }
}
local ThemeBtnsMap = {}
local function RefreshDesignButtons()
	local theme = GetTheme()
	local curD = Config.GuiDesign or "Default"
	for id, btn in pairs(DesignButtons) do
		local active = (id == curD)
		FUI.TweenButtonState(btn, active and theme.Accent or theme.PanelBg, active and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or theme.TextPrimary, active)
		btn.Text = (active and "[ON] " or "") .. (id == "Default" and "1. Default" or (id == "LiquidGlass" and "2. Liquid Glass" or "3. Cyber-HUD"))
	end
	if ThemeBtnsMap["Default"] then
		if curD == "LiquidGlass" then
			ThemeBtnsMap["Default"].Text = E.Bolt .. " Liquid Glass (Clear)"
			FUI.TweenButtonState(ThemeBtnsMap["Default"], Color3.fromRGB(70, 165, 255))
		elseif curD == "CyberHud" then
			ThemeBtnsMap["Default"].Text = E.Bolt .. " Cyber Neon (Default)"
			FUI.TweenButtonState(ThemeBtnsMap["Default"], Color3.fromRGB(0, 210, 235))
		else
			ThemeBtnsMap["Default"].Text = E.Bolt .. " T.R Default"
			FUI.TweenButtonState(ThemeBtnsMap["Default"], Themes["Default"].Accent)
		end
	end
end
table.insert(ThemeObjects.CustomCallbacks, RefreshDesignButtons)
for idx, dOpt in ipairs(DesignOptions) do
	local DBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
	DBtn.LayoutOrder = idx DBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold DBtn.TextSize = 11
	DBtn.Text = dOpt.Label DBtn.Parent = DesignCard AddCorner(DBtn, 6)
	FUI.AttachSpringAndRipple(DBtn)
	DesignButtons[dOpt.Id] = DBtn
	DBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if genv._TR_ApplyGuiDesign then genv._TR_ApplyGuiDesign(dOpt.Id, true) end
	end)
end

SetMouseLockState = function(newState) local prevML = CursorUnlockedOverride CursorUnlockedOverride = newState and true or false if prevML ~= CursorUnlockedOverride and not isClosingPermanently then NotifyFeatureToggle("Mouse Lock", CursorUnlockedOverride) end
	RefreshMouseLockUI() if CursorUnlockedOverride then pcall(function() player.DevEnableMouseLock = true
			UserSettings():GetService("UserGameSettings").RotationType = Enum.RotationType.CameraRelative
			UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCenter end) else pcall(function()
			UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = true
			UserSettings():GetService("UserGameSettings").RotationType = Enum.RotationType.MovementRelative end)
		pcall(function() local hum = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if hum then hum.AutoRotate = true hum.CameraOffset = V3_ZERO end end) end end
mouseLockToggleBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() SetMouseLockState(not CursorUnlockedOverride)
end) mouseLockKeyAssignBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	mouseLockKeyAssignBtn.Text = "[Key / Del]" ActiveKeybindCaptureCallback = function(keyName)
		TryAssignUniqueKeybind(keyName, "MouseLock", function(val) Config.MouseLockKeybind = val end, RefreshMouseLockUI, mouseLockKeyAssignBtn) end end)
mouseLockKeyDeleteBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Config.MouseLockKeybind = "None"
	ActiveKeybindCaptureCallback = nil RefreshMouseLockUI() SaveConfig(false) end) RefreshMouseLockUI()

CreateSectionHeader(Tab1_Dashboard, "Performance Booster & Floating Telemetry HUD", 16)
do
	local fpsOrigLighting = nil
	local fpsOrigTerrain = nil
	local fpsOrigEffects = {}
	local fpsOrigParticles = {}
	local fpsOrigParts = {}
	local fpsAddedConn = nil

	local function IsInAnyPlayerCharacter(inst)
		for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if plr.Character and inst:IsDescendantOf(plr.Character) then return true end
		end
		return false
	end

	local function OptimizeSingleInstance(inst)
		if not inst or not inst.Parent then return end
		if inst:IsA("PostEffect") then
			if inst.Name == "TR_LiquidGlassBlur" then return end
			if fpsOrigEffects[inst] == nil then fpsOrigEffects[inst] = inst.Enabled end
			pcall(function() inst.Enabled = false end)
		elseif inst:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or inst:IsA("Trail") or inst:IsA("Beam") or inst:IsA("Fire") or inst:IsA("Smoke") or inst:IsA("Sparkles") then
			if fpsOrigParticles[inst] == nil then fpsOrigParticles[inst] = inst.Enabled end
			pcall(function() inst.Enabled = false end)
		elseif inst:IsA("BasePart") and inst.Name ~= "TR_FakeFloor" and not inst:IsA("Terrain") then
			if IsInAnyPlayerCharacter(inst) then return end
			if not fpsOrigParts[inst] then
				fpsOrigParts[inst] = { Material = inst.Material, CastShadow = inst.CastShadow, Reflectance = inst.Reflectance }
			end
			pcall(function()
				inst.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
				inst.CastShadow = false
				inst.Reflectance = 0
			end)
		end
	end

	FUI.ApplyFpsBooster = function()
		if not fpsOrigLighting then
			fpsOrigLighting = {
				GlobalShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows,
				ShadowSoftness = Lighting.ShadowSoftness
			}
		end
		pcall(function()
			Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
			Lighting.ShadowSoftness = 0
		end)
		local terrain = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
		if terrain then
			if not fpsOrigTerrain then
				fpsOrigTerrain = {
					WaterWaveSize = terrain.WaterWaveSize,
					WaterWaveSpeed = terrain.WaterWaveSpeed,
					WaterReflectance = terrain.WaterReflectance,
					WaterTransparency = terrain.WaterTransparency
				}
			end
			pcall(function()
				terrain.WaterWaveSize = 0
				terrain.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
				terrain.WaterReflectance = 0
				terrain.WaterTransparency = 0
			end)
		end
		pcall(function()
			for _, desc in ipairs(Lighting:GetDescendants()) do
				if desc:IsA("PostEffect") then OptimizeSingleInstance(desc) end
			end
		end)
		task.spawn(function()
			local list = workspace:GetDescendants()
			for i = 1, #list do
				if not Config.FpsBoosterEnabled or isClosingPermanently then break end
				OptimizeSingleInstance(list[i])
				if i % 350 == 0 then RunService.Heartbeat:Wait() end
			end
		end)
		if not fpsAddedConn then
			fpsAddedConn = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(desc)
				if Config.FpsBoosterEnabled and not isClosingPermanently then
					OptimizeSingleInstance(desc)
				end
			end)
			FUI._FpsAddedConn = fpsAddedConn
		end
	end

	FUI.RestoreFpsBooster = function()
		if fpsAddedConn then
			pcall(function() fpsAddedConn:Disconnect() end)
			fpsAddedConn = nil
			FUI._FpsAddedConn = nil
		end
		if fpsOrigLighting then
			pcall(function()
				if not Config.FullbrightEnabled then
					Lighting.GlobalShadows = fpsOrigLighting.GlobalShadows
				end
				Lighting.ShadowSoftness = fpsOrigLighting.ShadowSoftness
			end)
			fpsOrigLighting = nil
		end
		local terrain = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
		if terrain and fpsOrigTerrain then
			pcall(function()
				terrain.WaterWaveSize = fpsOrigTerrain.WaterWaveSize
				terrain.WaterWaveSpeed = fpsOrigTerrain.WaterWaveSpeed
				terrain.WaterReflectance = fpsOrigTerrain.WaterReflectance
				terrain.WaterTransparency = fpsOrigTerrain.WaterTransparency
			end)
			fpsOrigTerrain = nil
		end
		for eff, origEn in pairs(fpsOrigEffects) do
			if eff and eff.Parent then pcall(function() eff.Enabled = origEn end) end
		end
		table.clear(fpsOrigEffects)
		for pt, origEn in pairs(fpsOrigParticles) do
			if pt and pt.Parent then pcall(function() pt.Enabled = origEn end) end
		end
		table.clear(fpsOrigParticles)
		for part, orig in pairs(fpsOrigParts) do
			if part and part.Parent then
				pcall(function()
					part.Material = orig.Material
					part.CastShadow = orig.CastShadow
					part.Reflectance = orig.Reflectance
				end)
			end
		end
		table.clear(fpsOrigParts)
	end

	CreateToggle(Tab1_Dashboard, "FPS Booster & Potato Mode (Lowers Textures/Particles/Shadows)", Config.FpsBoosterEnabled == true, 17, function(state)
		Config.FpsBoosterEnabled = state
		savedConfig.Settings.FpsBoosterEnabled = state
		if state then FUI.ApplyFpsBooster() else FUI.RestoreFpsBooster() end
		SaveConfig(false)
	end)
	if Config.FpsBoosterEnabled == true then task.defer(FUI.ApplyFpsBooster) end
end

do
	local StatsService = game:GetService("Stats")
	local savedW = tonumber(Config.TelemetryHudWidth) or 296
	local savedH = tonumber(Config.TelemetryHudHeight) or 174
	if savedW < 260 then savedW = 296 end
	if savedH < 156 then savedH = 174 end
	local hudW = math.clamp(savedW, 256, 460)
	local hudH = math.clamp(savedH, 156, 260)
	local hudLocked = (Config.TelemetryHudLocked == true)
	local hudCompact = false
	local hudExpandedH = hudH
	local lastCoordsStr = "0, 0, 0"
	local lastBadgesKey = nil

	local TelemetryHud = Instance.new("Frame")
	TelemetryHud.Name = "TR_TelemetryHUD"
	TelemetryHud.Size = UDim2.new(0, hudW, 0, hudH)
	TelemetryHud.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 1, -(hudH + 18))
	TelemetryHud.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	TelemetryHud.BackgroundTransparency = 0.06

	local hudStrokeGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient", hudStroke)
	hudStrokeGrad.Rotation = 25

	local hudHeader = Instance.new("Frame", TelemetryHud)
	hudHeader.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 28)
	hudHeader.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 6)
	hudHeader.BackgroundTransparency = 0.18
	hudHeader.BorderSizePixel = 0
	hudHeader.Active = true
	hudHeader.ZIndex = 61
	RegisterThemeObject(hudHeader, "BackgroundColor3", "PanelBg")
	AddCorner(hudHeader, 8)

	local hudStatusDot = Instance.new("Frame", hudHeader)
	hudStatusDot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 8)
	hudStatusDot.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0.5, -4)
	hudStatusDot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(52, 228, 134)
	hudStatusDot.BorderSizePixel = 0
	hudStatusDot.ZIndex = 62
	AddCorner(hudStatusDot, 4)

	local hudTitle = MakeLabel(hudHeader, UDim2.new(1, -118, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0, 21, 0, 0), E.Bolt .. " T.R TELEMETRY", Enum.Font.GothamBlack, 10, "Accent", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left)
	hudTitle.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
	hudTitle.ZIndex = 62

	local hudUptimeLbl = MakeLabel(hudHeader, UDim2.new(0, 48, 1, 0), UDim2.new(1, -128, 0, 0), "00:00", Enum.Font.Code, 10, "TextSecondary", Enum.TextXAlignment.Right)
	hudUptimeLbl.ZIndex = 62

	local hudCompactBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", hudHeader)

	local fpsTitleLbl = MakeLabel(fpsCard, UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 14), UDim2.new(0, 7, 0, 4), "FPS", Enum.Font.GothamBold, 9, "TextSecondary", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left)
	fpsTitleLbl.ZIndex = 63
	local fpsValLbl = MakeLabel(fpsCard, UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 20), UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 2), "60", Enum.Font.GothamBlack, 15, "TextPrimary", Enum.TextXAlignment.Right)

	memLbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd

	local plrLbl = MakeLabel(netCard, UDim2.new(1, -14, 0, 14), UDim2.new(0, 7, 0, 39), "PLR: 1  |  SPD: 0", Enum.Font.Code, 10, "Accent", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left)
	plrLbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
	plrLbl.ZIndex = 63

	local posStrip = Instance.new("Frame", hudBody)
	posStrip.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26)

	local hudCoordsLbl = MakeLabel(posStrip, UDim2.new(1, -36, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0), "XYZ: --, --, --", Enum.Font.Code, 10, "TextPrimary", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left)
	hudCoordsLbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
	hudCoordsLbl.ZIndex = 63

	local hudCopyPosBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", posStrip)
	hudCopyPosBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 18)
	hudCopyPosBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0.5, -9)
	hudCopyPosBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.12
	hudCopyPosBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
	hudCopyPosBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	hudCopyPosBtn.TextSize = 10
	hudCopyPosBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	hudCopyPosBtn.Text = E.Clip
	hudCopyPosBtn.ZIndex = 64
	RegisterThemeObject(hudCopyPosBtn, "BackgroundColor3", "Accent")
	AddCorner(hudCopyPosBtn, 5)
	hudCopyPosBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		SafeClipboard(lastCoordsStr)
		hudCopyPosBtn.Text = E.Check
		task.delay(0.9, function()
			if hudCopyPosBtn and hudCopyPosBtn.Parent then hudCopyPosBtn.Text = E.Clip end
		end)
	end)

	local badgesHolder = Instance.new("Frame", hudBody)
	badgesHolder.Size = UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 24)
	badgesHolder.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 94)
	badgesHolder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	badgesHolder.ClipsDescendants = true
	badgesHolder.ZIndex = 62
	local badgesLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", badgesHolder)
	badgesLayout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
	badgesLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
	badgesLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)

	local hudResizeHandle = Instance.new("TextButton", TelemetryHud)
	hudResizeHandle.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
	hudResizeHandle.Position = UDim2.new(1, -17, 1, -17)
	hudResizeHandle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	hudResizeHandle.BorderSizePixel = 0
	hudResizeHandle.Text = "◢"
	hudResizeHandle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	hudResizeHandle.TextSize = 11
	hudResizeHandle.ZIndex = 65
	RegisterThemeObject(hudResizeHandle, "TextColor3", "Accent")

	local function RebuildTelemetryBadges(badgesText)
		local key = tostring(badgesText or "")
		if key == lastBadgesKey then return end
		lastBadgesKey = key
		for _, ch in ipairs(badgesHolder:GetChildren()) do
			if ch:IsA("TextLabel") then ch:Destroy() end
		end
		local th = GetTheme()
		if key == "" then
			local idlePill = Instance.new("TextLabel", badgesHolder)
			idlePill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 52, 0, 18)
			idlePill.BackgroundColor3 = th.PanelBg
			idlePill.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
			idlePill.BorderSizePixel = 0
			idlePill.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
			idlePill.TextSize = 9
			idlePill.TextColor3 = th.TextSecondary
			idlePill.Text = "IDLE"
			idlePill.ZIndex = 63
			AddCorner(idlePill, 9)
			return
		end
		for token in string.gmatch(key, "[^|]+") do
			local trimmed = token:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
			if trimmed ~= "" then
				local pill = Instance.new("TextLabel", badgesHolder)
				pill.Size = UDim2.new(0, math.max(38, (#trimmed * 7) + 14), 0, 18)
				pill.BackgroundColor3 = th.Accent
				pill.BackgroundTransparency = 0.14
				pill.BorderSizePixel = 0
				pill.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
				pill.TextSize = 9
				pill.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
				pill.Text = trimmed
				pill.ZIndex = 63
				AddCorner(pill, 9)
			end
		end
	end

	local function SyncTelemetryLockVisual()
		local th = GetTheme()
		hudLockBtn.Text = hudLocked and E.Lock or E.Unlock
		hudLockBtn.BackgroundColor3 = hudLocked and th.Accent or th.CardBg
		hudResizeHandle.Visible = (not hudLocked) and (not hudCompact)
	end

	FUI.ApplyTelemetryHudStyle = function(activeTh)
		local th = activeTh or GetTheme()
		local d = Config.GuiDesign or "Default"
		TelemetryHud.BackgroundTransparency = (d == "LiquidGlass" and 0.28) or (d == "CyberHud" and 0.04) or 0.06
		hudBgGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, th.MainBg:Lerp(th.Accent, 0.12)),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, th.MainBg),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, th.MainBg:Lerp(th.Stroke, 0.14))
		})
		hudStrokeGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, th.Accent),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, th.Stroke)
		})
		hudCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, (d == "LiquidGlass" and 16) or (d == "CyberHud" and 6) or 13)
		hudStroke.Thickness = (d == "CyberHud" and 2.1) or 1.7
		hudHeader.BackgroundTransparency = (d == "LiquidGlass" and 0.42) or 0.18
		lastBadgesKey = nil
		SyncTelemetryLockVisual()
	end
	table.insert(ThemeObjects.CustomCallbacks, FUI.ApplyTelemetryHudStyle)

	hudCompactBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		hudCompact = not hudCompact
		hudCompactBtn.Text = hudCompact and "▴" or "▾"
		hudBody.Visible = not hudCompact
		hudResizeHandle.Visible = (not hudLocked) and (not hudCompact)
		if not hudCompact then
			hudTitle.Text = E.Bolt .. " T.R TELEMETRY"
		end
		FUI.SafeTween(TelemetryHud, FUI.TI.TabPage, {
			Size = UDim2.new(0, TelemetryHud.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, hudCompact and 40 or hudExpandedH)
		})
	end)

	local hudDragging, hudDragStart, hudStartPos = false, nil, nil
	local hudResizing, hudResizeStart, hudStartSize = false, nil, nil
	hudHeader.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
		if hudLocked then return end
		if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			hudDragging = true
			hudDragStart = inp.Position
			hudStartPos = TelemetryHud.Position
			inp.Changed:Connect(function()
				if inp.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then hudDragging = false end
			end)
		end
	end)
	hudResizeHandle.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
		if hudLocked or hudCompact then return end
		if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			hudResizing = true
			hudResizeStart = inp.Position
			hudStartSize = TelemetryHud.AbsoluteSize
			inp.Changed:Connect(function()
				if inp.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
					hudResizing = false
					Config.TelemetryHudWidth = math.floor(TelemetryHud.AbsoluteSize.X + 0.5)
					Config.TelemetryHudHeight = math.floor(TelemetryHud.AbsoluteSize.Y + 0.5)
					hudExpandedH = Config.TelemetryHudHeight
					savedConfig.Settings.TelemetryHudWidth = Config.TelemetryHudWidth
					savedConfig.Settings.TelemetryHudHeight = Config.TelemetryHudHeight
					SaveConfig(false)
				end
			end)
		end
	end)
	table.insert(Connections, UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
		if inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
		if hudLocked then hudDragging = false hudResizing = false return end
		if hudResizing and hudResizeStart and hudStartSize then
			local d = inp.Position - hudResizeStart
			local nw = math.clamp(math.floor(hudStartSize.X + d.X + 0.5), 256, 460)
			local nh = math.clamp(math.floor(hudStartSize.Y + d.Y + 0.5), 156, 260)
			hudExpandedH = nh
			TelemetryHud.Size = UDim2.new(0, nw, 0, nh)
		elseif hudDragging and hudDragStart and hudStartPos then
			local d = inp.Position - hudDragStart
			local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
			local vs = cam and cam.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1280, 720)
			local curW, curH = TelemetryHud.AbsoluteSize.X, TelemetryHud.AbsoluteSize.Y
			local rawX = (hudStartPos.X.Scale * vs.X) + hudStartPos.X.Offset + d.X
			local rawY = (hudStartPos.Y.Scale * vs.Y) + hudStartPos.Y.Offset + d.Y
			local cx = math.clamp(rawX, 4, math.max(4, vs.X - curW - 4))
			local cy = math.clamp(rawY, 4, math.max(4, vs.Y - curH - 4))
			TelemetryHud.Position = UDim2.new(0, cx, 0, cy)
		end
	end))

	hudLockBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		hudLocked = not hudLocked
		Config.TelemetryHudLocked = hudLocked
		savedConfig.Settings.TelemetryHudLocked = hudLocked
		if hudLocked then hudDragging = false hudResizing = false end
		SyncTelemetryLockVisual()
		SaveConfig(false)
		NotifyFeatureToggle("Telemetry HUD Position Lock", hudLocked)
	end)

	local hudToggleCtrl = nil
	hudHideBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		Config.TelemetryHudEnabled = false
		savedConfig.Settings.TelemetryHudEnabled = false
		TelemetryHud.Visible = false
		if hudToggleCtrl then hudToggleCtrl.SetState(false, false) end
		SaveConfig(false)
		NotifyFeatureToggle("Floating Telemetry HUD", false)
	end)

	hudToggleCtrl = CreateToggle(Tab1_Dashboard, "Floating Mini Telemetry HUD (FPS, Ping, XYZ & Badges | Drag/Resize/Lock)", Config.TelemetryHudEnabled == true, 18, function(state)
		Config.TelemetryHudEnabled = state
		savedConfig.Settings.TelemetryHudEnabled = state
		TelemetryHud.Visible = state and not isClosingPermanently
		SaveConfig(false)
	end)
	CreateToggle(Tab1_Dashboard, "Toggle Feature Notifications (Animated Toast Alerts)", Config.ToggleNotifsEnabled ~= false, 19, function(state)
		Config.ToggleNotifsEnabled = state
		savedConfig.Settings.ToggleNotifsEnabled = state
		if not state and FUI.ClearAllNotifications then pcall(FUI.ClearAllNotifications) end
		SaveConfig(false)
	end)

	FUI.TelemetryHudFrame = TelemetryHud
	FUI.UpdateTelemetryHud = function(fpsVal, pingMs, rootPart, badgesText)
		if not TelemetryHud or not TelemetryHud.Visible or isClosingPermanently or FUI.IsWindowUnfocused then return end
		local fpsNum = tonumber(fpsVal) or 0
		local pingNum = tonumber(pingMs) or 0
		local fpsCol = (fpsNum >= 50 and Color3.fromRGB(52, 228, 134))
			or (fpsNum >= 30 and Color3.fromRGB(255, 198, 66))
			or Color3.fromRGB(245, 82, 92)
		local pingCol = (pingNum <= 85 and Color3.fromRGB(52, 228, 134))
			or (pingNum <= 160 and Color3.fromRGB(255, 198, 66))
			or Color3.fromRGB(245, 82, 92)
		hudStatusDot.BackgroundColor3 = fpsCol

		local elapsed = math.max(0, math.floor(os.clock() - (FUI.SessionStartClock or os.clock())))
		local mins = math.floor(elapsed / 60)
		local secs = elapsed % 60
		hudUptimeLbl.Text = string.format("%02d:%02d", mins, secs)

		local spd = 0
		if rootPart then
			local p = rootPart.Position
			lastCoordsStr = string.format("%.0f, %.0f, %.0f", p.X, p.Y, p.Z)
			hudCoordsLbl.Text = "XYZ: " .. lastCoordsStr
			local okV, vel = pcall(function() return rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity end)
			if okV and vel then spd = math.floor(vel.Magnitude + 0.5) end
		else
			hudCoordsLbl.Text = "XYZ: --, --, --"
		end

		if hudCompact then
			hudTitle.Text = string.format("%s %d FPS | %d ms | %d s/s", E.Bolt, fpsNum, pingNum, spd)
			return
		end

		fpsValLbl.Text = tostring(fpsNum)
		fpsValLbl.TextColor3 = fpsCol
		pingValLbl.Text = string.format("%d ms", pingNum)
		pingValLbl.TextColor3 = pingCol

		table.remove(sparkHistory, 1)
		table.insert(sparkHistory, fpsNum)
		for si = 1, SPARK_COUNT do
			local v = sparkHistory[si] or 0
			local ratio = math.clamp(v / 60, 0.14, 1.0)
			local bar = sparkBars[si]
			bar.Size = UDim2.new(1 / SPARK_COUNT, -2, ratio, 0)
			bar.BackgroundColor3 = (v >= 50 and Color3.fromRGB(52, 228, 134))
				or (v >= 30 and Color3.fromRGB(255, 198, 66))
				or Color3.fromRGB(245, 82, 92)
		end

		local okMem, memMb = pcall(function() return StatsService:GetTotalMemoryUsageMb() end)
		memLbl.Text = (okMem and type(memMb) == "number") and string.format("MEM: %.0f MB", memMb) or "MEM: -- MB"
		plrLbl.Text = string.format("PLR: %d/%d | SPD: %d", #Players:GetPlayers(), Players.MaxPlayers or 0, spd)
		RebuildTelemetryBadges(badgesText)
	end
	FUI.ApplyTelemetryHudStyle()
end

CreateSectionHeader(Tab1_Dashboard, "Master Keybind Overview Panel (Edit, Delete or Reset All Shortcuts)", 20)
do
	local kbExpanded = false
	local KeybindCard = CreateCard(Tab1_Dashboard, 44, 21)
	KeybindCard.ClipsDescendants = true

	local kbHeaderBtn = MakeButton(KeybindCard, UDim2.new(0.68, -14, 0, 28), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 8), E.Bolt .. " Master Keybinds Overview [Expand]", "PanelBg", 11)
	local kbResetAllBtn = MakeButton(KeybindCard, UDim2.new(0.32, -10, 0, 28), UDim2.new(0.68, 2, 0, 8), E.Refresh .. " Reset Defaults", "AccentDark", 11)

	local kbListHolder = Instance.new("Frame", KeybindCard)
	kbListHolder.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 282)
	kbListHolder.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 44)
	kbListHolder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	kbListHolder.Visible = false
	local kbLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", kbListHolder)
	kbLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 5)
	kbLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder

	local keybindSpecs = {
		{ Label = "Mouse Lock / Shift Lock", Slot = "MouseLock", Key = "MouseLockKeybind", Default = "M" },
		{ Label = "Flight Toggle", Slot = "Flight", Key = "FlyKeybind", Default = "E" },
		{ Label = "Freecam Explorer", Slot = "Freecam", Key = "FreecamKeybind", Default = "C" },
		{ Label = "Normal Aimbot", Slot = "Aimbot", Key = "AimbotKeybind", Default = "Q" },
		{ Label = "Target Player Aimbot", Slot = "TargetAimbot", Key = "TargetAimbotKeybind", Default = "T" },
		{ Label = "Auto-Shoot / Triggerbot", Slot = "AutoShoot", Key = "AutoShootKeybind", Default = "Z" },
		{ Label = "Radial Quick-Action Wheel (Hold)", Slot = "RadialWheel", Key = "RadialWheelKeybind", Default = "V" }
	}
	local kbRowButtons = {}

	local function RefreshMasterKeybindPanel()
		for _, spec in ipairs(keybindSpecs) do
			local btn = kbRowButtons[spec.Slot]
			if btn and btn.Parent then
				btn.Text = "[" .. tostring(Config[spec.Key] or "None") .. "]"
			end
		end
	end
	FUI.RefreshMasterKeybindPanel = RefreshMasterKeybindPanel

	local function SyncAllHubKeybindDisplays()
		RefreshMasterKeybindPanel()
		if RefreshMouseLockUI then pcall(RefreshMouseLockUI) end
		if FUI.RefreshFlightUI then pcall(FUI.RefreshFlightUI) end
		if FUI.RefreshFreecamUI then pcall(FUI.RefreshFreecamUI) end
		if FUI.RefreshAimbotUI then pcall(FUI.RefreshAimbotUI) end
		if FUI.RefreshRadialKeyUI then pcall(FUI.RefreshRadialKeyUI) end
	end

	for idx, spec in ipairs(keybindSpecs) do
		local r = Instance.new("Frame", kbListHolder)
		r.LayoutOrder = idx
		r.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
		r.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
		r.BorderSizePixel = 0
		RegisterThemeObject(r, "BackgroundColor3", "PanelBg")
		AddCorner(r, 6)
		local l = MakeLabel(r, UDim2.new(1, -148, 1, 0), UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0), spec.Label, Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, 11, "TextPrimary", Enum.TextXAlignment.Left)
		l.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
		local bBtn = MakeButton(r, UDim2.new(0, 74, 0, 22), UDim2.new(1, -138, 0.5, -11), "[" .. tostring(Config[spec.Key] or "None") .. "]", "CardBg", 10)
		kbRowButtons[spec.Slot] = bBtn
		bBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			bBtn.Text = "[Key/Del]"
			ActiveKeybindCaptureCallback = function(k)
				TryAssignUniqueKeybind(k, spec.Slot, function(val)
					Config[spec.Key] = val
					savedConfig.Settings[spec.Key] = val
				end, SyncAllHubKeybindDisplays, bBtn)
			end
		end)
		local dBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", r)
		dBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 52, 0, 22)
		dBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -58, 0.5, -11)
		dBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(165, 38, 55)
		dBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
		dBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		dBtn.TextSize = 10
		dBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		dBtn.Text = "Clear"
		AddCorner(dBtn, 5)
		FUI.AttachSpringAndRipple(dBtn)
		dBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			Config[spec.Key] = "None"
			savedConfig.Settings[spec.Key] = "None"
			ActiveKeybindCaptureCallback = nil
			SyncAllHubKeybindDisplays()
			SaveConfig(false)
		end)
	end

	local fixedInfo = MakeLabel(kbListHolder, UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20), nil, "Fixed Shortcuts: [X] Toggle Hub  |  [F4] Hacker Detector  |  [Del] Unbind", Enum.Font.Gotham, 10, "TextSecondary", Enum.TextXAlignment.Center)
	fixedInfo.LayoutOrder = 99

	kbHeaderBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		kbExpanded = not kbExpanded
		kbHeaderBtn.Text = E.Bolt .. " Master Keybinds Overview [" .. (kbExpanded and "Collapse" or "Expand") .. "]"
		kbListHolder.Visible = kbExpanded
		FUI.SafeTween(KeybindCard, FUI.TI.TabPage, {
			Size = kbExpanded and UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 332) or UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44)
		})
		if kbExpanded then RefreshMasterKeybindPanel() end
	end)

	kbResetAllBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		for _, spec in ipairs(keybindSpecs) do
			Config[spec.Key] = spec.Default
			savedConfig.Settings[spec.Key] = spec.Default
		end
		ActiveKeybindCaptureCallback = nil
		SyncAllHubKeybindDisplays()
		SaveConfig(false)
		NotifyFeatureToggle("All Keybinds Reset to Defaults", true)
	end)
end

	CenterAura.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)

	AddCorner(CenterAura, 61)

	CenterStar.ZIndex = 115

	CenterTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0, 34)
	CenterTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0, 7, 0, 26)
	CenterTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	CenterTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	CenterTitle.TextSize = 11
	CenterTitle.TextWrapped = true
	CenterTitle.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
	CenterTitle.TextColor3 = GetTheme().TextPrimary
	CenterTitle.Text = "Quick Wheel"
	CenterTitle.ZIndex = 115

	local CenterPill = Instance.new("TextLabel", CenterCore)
	CenterPill.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
	CenterPill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 80, 0, 18)
	CenterPill.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 68)
	CenterPill.BackgroundColor3 = GetTheme().Accent
	CenterPill.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
	CenterPill.BorderSizePixel = 0
	CenterPill.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
	CenterPill.TextSize = 9
	CenterPill.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	CenterPill.Text = "TOP 8 FAVS"
	CenterPill.ZIndex = 115
	AddCorner(CenterPill, 9)

	local wheelSlots = {}
	local wheelItems = {}
	local hoveredSlotIdx = nil
	local radialOpen = false
	local wheelCenterPos = Vector2.new(0, 0)
	local radialMoveConn = nil

	for i = 1, 8 do
		local angle = ((i - 1) * (math.pi / 4)) - (math.pi / 2)
		local ox = math.floor(math.cos(angle) * SLOT_RADIUS + 0.5)
		local oy = math.floor(math.sin(angle) * SLOT_RADIUS + 0.5)
		local node = Instance.new("TextButton", WheelRoot)
		node.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		node.Size = UDim2.new(0, 96, 0, 42)
		node.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, ox, 0.5, oy)
		node.BackgroundColor3 = GetTheme().PanelBg
		node.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
		node.BorderSizePixel = 0
		node.Text = ""
		node.AutoButtonColor = false
		node.ZIndex = 112
		AddCorner(node, 11)
		local nScale = Instance.new("UIScale", node)
		nScale.Scale = 1.0
		local nStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", node)
		nStroke.Thickness = 1.4
		nStroke.Transparency = 0.25
		nStroke.Color = GetTheme().Stroke

		local numBadge = Instance.new("TextLabel", node)
		numBadge.Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18)
		numBadge.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0.5, -9)
		numBadge.BackgroundColor3 = GetTheme().CardBg
		numBadge.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
		numBadge.BorderSizePixel = 0
		numBadge.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
		numBadge.TextSize = 9
		numBadge.TextColor3 = GetTheme().Accent
		numBadge.Text = tostring(i)
		numBadge.ZIndex = 113
		AddCorner(numBadge, 9)

		local dot = Instance.new("Frame", node)
		dot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 7, 0, 7)
		dot.Position = UDim2.new(1, -13, 0, 6)
		dot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(115, 125, 140)
		dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
		dot.ZIndex = 113
		AddCorner(dot, 4)

		local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", node)
		lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -38, 1, -6)
		lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 3)
		lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		lbl.TextSize = 10
		lbl.TextWrapped = true
		lbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
		lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		lbl.TextColor3 = GetTheme().TextPrimary
		lbl.Text = "Empty"
		lbl.ZIndex = 113

		wheelSlots[i] = { Node = node, Scale = nScale, Stroke = nStroke, Badge = numBadge, Dot = dot, Label = lbl, Angle = angle }
		node.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			local item = wheelItems[i]
			if item and item.Reg and item.Reg.Toggle then
				pcall(item.Reg.Toggle)
				if FUI.CloseRadialWheel then FUI.CloseRadialWheel(false) end
			end
		end)
	end

	local function ApplyRadialTheme(th)
		th = th or GetTheme()
		local d = Config.GuiDesign or "Default"
		WheelRoot.BackgroundTransparency = (d == "LiquidGlass" and 0.30) or (d == "CyberHud" and 0.08) or 0.14
		WheelBgGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, th.MainBg:Lerp(th.Accent, 0.14)),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, th.MainBg),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, th.MainBg:Lerp(th.Stroke, 0.16))
		})
		WheelStrokeGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, th.Accent),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, th.Stroke)
		})
		OrbitStroke.Color = th.Accent
		CenterAuraStroke.Color = th.Accent
		CenterBgGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, th.CardBg:Lerp(th.Accent, 0.18)),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, th.MainBg)
		})
		CenterStroke.Color = th.Accent
		CenterTitle.TextColor3 = th.TextPrimary
		CenterPill.BackgroundColor3 = th.Accent
		for i = 1, 8 do
			local s = wheelSlots[i]
			s.Label.TextColor3 = th.TextPrimary
			s.Badge.BackgroundColor3 = th.CardBg
			s.Badge.TextColor3 = th.Accent
			if hoveredSlotIdx ~= i then
				s.Node.BackgroundColor3 = th.PanelBg
				s.Stroke.Color = th.Stroke
			end
		end
	end
	table.insert(ThemeObjects.CustomCallbacks, ApplyRadialTheme)

	local function UpdateRadialHover(idx)
		if hoveredSlotIdx == idx then return end
		local th = GetTheme()
		if hoveredSlotIdx and wheelSlots[hoveredSlotIdx] then
			local prev = wheelSlots[hoveredSlotIdx]
			prev.Node.BackgroundColor3 = th.PanelBg
			prev.Stroke.Color = th.Stroke
			prev.Stroke.Thickness = 1.4
			prev.Stroke.Transparency = 0.25
			prev.Badge.BackgroundColor3 = th.CardBg
			prev.Badge.TextColor3 = th.Accent
			FUI.SafeTween(prev.Scale, FUI.TI.HoverOut, { Scale = 1.0 })
		end
		hoveredSlotIdx = idx
		if idx and wheelSlots[idx] and wheelItems[idx] then
			local cur = wheelSlots[idx]
			local item = wheelItems[idx]
			cur.Node.BackgroundColor3 = th.Accent
			cur.Stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			cur.Stroke.Thickness = 2.2
			cur.Stroke.Transparency = 0.0
			cur.Badge.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			cur.Badge.TextColor3 = th.MainBg
			FUI.SafeTween(cur.Scale, FUI.TI.HoverIn, { Scale = 1.12 })
			local isOn = item.Reg.GetState and (item.Reg.GetState() == true)
			CenterTitle.Text = item.ShortName
			CenterPill.Text = isOn and "STATUS: ON" or "STATUS: OFF"
			CenterPill.BackgroundColor3 = isOn and Color3.fromRGB(40, 212, 122) or Color3.fromRGB(235, 76, 88)
		else
			CenterTitle.Text = (#wheelItems > 0) and "Quick Wheel" or "No Favorites"
			CenterPill.Text = (#wheelItems > 0) and "RELEASE KEY" or "PIN ★ IN TABS"
			CenterPill.BackgroundColor3 = th.Accent
		end
	end

	local function PopulateRadialSlots()
		table.clear(wheelItems)
		local th = GetTheme()
		local idx = 0
		for _, funcName in ipairs(FUI.FavoriteOrder) do
			if FUI.IsFunctionFavorited(funcName) then
				local reg = FUI.FavoriteRegistry[funcName]
				if reg then
					idx = idx + 1
					local shortName = tostring(reg.Name or funcName):gsub("%s*%b()$", "")
					if shortName == "" then shortName = tostring(reg.Name or funcName) end
					wheelItems[idx] = { Name = funcName, ShortName = shortName, Reg = reg }
					if idx >= 8 then break end
				end
			end
		end
		for i = 1, 8 do
			local s = wheelSlots[i]
			local item = wheelItems[i]
			s.Node.BackgroundColor3 = th.PanelBg
			s.Stroke.Color = th.Stroke
			s.Stroke.Thickness = 1.4
			s.Stroke.Transparency = 0.25
			s.Badge.BackgroundColor3 = th.CardBg
			s.Badge.TextColor3 = th.Accent
			s.Scale.Scale = 1.0
			if item then
				local isOn = item.Reg.GetState and (item.Reg.GetState() == true)
				s.Node.BackgroundTransparency = 0.06
				s.Label.Text = item.ShortName
				s.Label.TextTransparency = 0
				s.Badge.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
				s.Badge.TextTransparency = 0
				s.Dot.BackgroundColor3 = isOn and Color3.fromRGB(48, 228, 134) or Color3.fromRGB(140, 148, 165)
			else
				s.Node.BackgroundTransparency = 0.58
				s.Label.Text = "Empty"
				s.Label.TextTransparency = 0.58
				s.Badge.BackgroundTransparency = 0.58
				s.Badge.TextTransparency = 0.55
				s.Dot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 88, 104)
			end
		end
		hoveredSlotIdx = nil
		CenterTitle.Text = (#wheelItems > 0) and "Quick Wheel" or "No Favorites"
		CenterPill.Text = (#wheelItems > 0) and "MOVE & RELEASE" or "PIN ★ IN TABS"
		CenterPill.BackgroundColor3 = th.Accent
	end

	FUI.OpenRadialWheel = function()
		if radialOpen or isClosingPermanently or Config.RadialWheelEnabled == false then return end
		radialOpen = true
		ApplyRadialTheme(GetTheme())
		PopulateRadialSlots()
		local mLoc = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
		local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
		local vs = cam and cam.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1280, 720)
		local margin = HALF_WHEEL + 8
		local cx = math.clamp(mLoc.X, margin, math.max(margin, vs.X - margin))
		local cy = math.clamp(mLoc.Y, margin, math.max(margin, vs.Y - margin))
		wheelCenterPos = Vector2.new(cx, cy)
		WheelRoot.Position = UDim2.new(0, cx, 0, cy)
		WheelScale.Scale = 0.72
		WheelRoot.Visible = true
		FUI.SafeTween(WheelScale, FUI.TI.NotifIn, { Scale = 1.0 })
		if radialMoveConn then pcall(function() radialMoveConn:Disconnect() end) end
		radialMoveConn = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
			if not radialOpen then return end
			if inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and inp.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
			local pos = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
			local dx = pos.X - wheelCenterPos.X
			local dy = pos.Y - wheelCenterPos.Y
			local dist = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
			if dist < 48 then
				UpdateRadialHover(nil)
				return
			end
			local ang = math.atan2(dy, dx) + (math.pi / 2)
			if ang < 0 then ang = ang + (math.pi * 2) end
			local sector = (math.floor(((ang + (math.pi / 8)) % (math.pi * 2)) / (math.pi / 4)) % 8) + 1
			if wheelItems[sector] then
				UpdateRadialHover(sector)
			else
				UpdateRadialHover(nil)
			end
		end)
		FUI._RadialMoveConn = radialMoveConn
	end

	FUI.CloseRadialWheel = function(triggerHovered)
		if not radialOpen then return end
		radialOpen = false
		if radialMoveConn then
			pcall(function() radialMoveConn:Disconnect() end)
			radialMoveConn = nil
			FUI._RadialMoveConn = nil
		end
		local chosen = (triggerHovered == true and hoveredSlotIdx) and wheelItems[hoveredSlotIdx] or nil
		hoveredSlotIdx = nil
		WheelRoot.Visible = false
		if chosen and chosen.Reg and chosen.Reg.Toggle then
			pcall(chosen.Reg.Toggle)
		end
	end
	ApplyRadialTheme(GetTheme())
end

CreateSectionHeader(Tab1_Dashboard, "Smart Anti-AFK, Session Uptime & Window-Focus Power Saver", 25)
do
	local UptimeCard = CreateCard(Tab1_Dashboard, 44, 26)

	pbLbl.ZIndex = 121
	table.insert(ThemeObjects.CustomCallbacks, function(th)
		PowerBanner.BackgroundColor3 = th.MainBg
		pbStroke.Color = th.Accent
	end)

	local function FormatUptime(sec)
		local s = math.max(0, math.floor(sec))
		local h = math.floor(s / 3600)
		local m = math.floor((s % 3600) / 60)
		local r = s % 60
		return string.format("%02d:%02d:%02d", h, m, r)
	end

	FUI.UpdateSessionUptimeLabel = function()
		if not uptimeLbl or not uptimeLbl.Parent then return end
		local elapsed = os.clock() - (FUI.SessionStartClock or os.clock())
		local focusTxt = FUI.IsWindowUnfocused and "POWER SAVER" or "ACTIVE"
		uptimeLbl.Text = string.format("Uptime: %s  |  AFK Saves: %d  |  Focus: %s", FormatUptime(elapsed), FUI.AntiAfkSaves or 0, focusTxt)
	end

	FUI.SetWindowPowerSaverState = function(unfocused)
		if isClosingPermanently then return end
		if unfocused and Config.FocusPowerSaverEnabled ~= false then
			FUI.IsWindowUnfocused = true
			PowerBackdrop.Visible = true
			PowerBanner.Visible = true
			pcall(function() RunService:Set3dRenderingEnabled(false) end)
		else
			FUI.IsWindowUnfocused = false
			PowerBackdrop.Visible = false
			PowerBanner.Visible = false
			pcall(function() RunService:Set3dRenderingEnabled(true) end)
		end
		FUI.UpdateSessionUptimeLabel()
	end

	CreateToggle(Tab1_Dashboard, "Window-Focus Power Saver (Pause 3D & UI Load When Alt-Tabbed)", Config.FocusPowerSaverEnabled ~= false, 27, function(state)
		Config.FocusPowerSaverEnabled = state
		savedConfig.Settings.FocusPowerSaverEnabled = state
		if not state then FUI.SetWindowPowerSaverState(false) end
		SaveConfig(false)
	end)

	PowerBackdrop.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		FUI.SetWindowPowerSaverState(false)
	end)
	PowerBanner.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		FUI.SetWindowPowerSaverState(false)
	end)
	table.insert(Connections, UserInputService.WindowFocusReleased:Connect(function()
		if FUI.CloseRadialWheel then pcall(FUI.CloseRadialWheel, false) end
		if Config.FocusPowerSaverEnabled ~= false and not isClosingPermanently then
			FUI.SetWindowPowerSaverState(true)
		end
	end))
	table.insert(Connections, UserInputService.WindowFocused:Connect(function()
		FUI.SetWindowPowerSaverState(false)
	end))
end
end if FUI.SetLoadProgress then FUI.SetLoadProgress(20, "Loading Themes & Status...") end SetupTab1()
